# Economics Form 5, Form Five Economics: authored answers for review

Every answer fixed by a person, not computed. Part 1: the tables, conventions and rules that computed answers come from.
Part 2: every authored question template, with all its data (keys, statements, pairs, choices and feedback) and sources.
Regenerate with `npm run review:f5-economics`. Item IDs (TR-…) match `docs/teacher-review.md`.

## Part 1

Rules the computed answers use are listed in the subject’s library tests; every authored answer is in Part 2.

### `n5-1-terms` · Lesson 1 · multiple choice, level 1

Prompt: {X.q}

Table `X` (one row is picked each time):

| q | r | w | e | y |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Which term means an accepted intermediary used in payment? | medium of exchange | unit of account · store of value · standard of deferred payment | unit of account concerns a common measure for expressing prices and debts, rather than the concept asked for. · store of value concerns a way of carrying purchasing ability over time, rather than the concept asked for. · standard of deferred payment concerns a unit for stating an obligation payable later, rather than the concept asked for. | An accepted intermediary used in payment. |
| Which term means a common measure for expressing prices and debts? | unit of account | medium of exchange · store of value · standard of deferred payment | medium of exchange concerns an accepted intermediary used in payment, rather than the concept asked for. · store of value concerns a way of carrying purchasing ability over time, rather than the concept asked for. · standard of deferred payment concerns a unit for stating an obligation payable later, rather than the concept asked for. | A common measure for expressing prices and debts. |

| Which term means a unit for stating an obligation payable later? | standard of deferred payment | medium of exchange · unit of account · store of value | medium of exchange concerns an accepted intermediary used in payment, rather than the concept asked for. · unit of account concerns a common measure for expressing prices and debts, rather than the concept asked for. · store of value concerns a way of carrying purchasing ability over time, rather than the concept asked for. | A unit for stating an obligation payable later. |

### `n5-1-apply` · Lesson 1 · multiple choice, level 2

Prompt: {X.q}

Table `X` (one row is picked each time):

| q | r | w | e | y |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Why can accepted currency help when the vegetable seller does not want notebooks? | It separates the sale from the later purchase. | It makes all goods equally priced. · It eliminates every production cost. · It guarantees no price changes. | A common medium does not equalise prices. · Exchange does not remove production costs. · Money does not guarantee stable prices. | The sellers no longer need matching immediate wants. |
| A pupil compares folder and notebook prices in CFA francs. Which function is central? | Unit of account. | Store of value only. · Physical durability. · An instruction to transfer a deposit. | The case is a price comparison, not retaining balances. · Durability is a quality, not this comparison function. · No payment instruction is described. | The common unit permits comparison. |
| A seller keeps a balance for next week. What qualification is needed? | Its later purchasing power may change. | It becomes worthless by definition overnight. · It must buy exactly the same basket forever. · It proves the seller owns no other wealth. | A possibility of change is not inevitable worthlessness. · Price changes can alter the basket. · A money balance says nothing complete about other assets. | Store of value is not a guarantee of constant real value. |
| Which distinction is correct? | A cheque instructs payment from a deposit; it is not identical to the deposit balance. | A cheque is necessarily central-bank cash. · Every valuable machine is immediately money. · A price label is itself a paid debt. | A cheque is a payment instrument. · Valuable wealth need not be broadly accepted in payment. · A label states a price rather than paying it. | Separate money, wealth and payment instructions. |

Right answer: `{X.r}`; wrong choices: `{X.w[0]}`, `{X.w[1]}`, `{X.w[2]}`

- Feedback `w0`: “{X.e[0]}”
- Feedback `w1`: “{X.e[1]}”
- Feedback `w2`: “{X.e[2]}”

Three generated variants:

> Which distinction is correct?

- ◻️ A cheque is necessarily central-bank cash.  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: A cheque is a payment instrument._
- ◻️ Every valuable machine is immediately money.  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: Valuable wealth need not be broadly accepted in payment._
- ◻️ A price label is itself a paid debt.  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: A label states a price rather than paying it._
- ✅ A cheque instructs payment from a deposit; it is not identical to the deposit balance.

Full working after a second miss:

> Separate money, wealth and payment instructions.  
> So the answer is “A cheque instructs payment from a deposit; it is not identical to the deposit balance.”.  

> Why can accepted currency help when the vegetable seller does not want notebooks?

- ◻️ It eliminates every production cost.  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: Exchange does not remove production costs._
- ◻️ It guarantees no price changes.  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: Money does not guarantee stable prices._
- ✅ It separates the sale from the later purchase.
- ◻️ It makes all goods equally priced.  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: A common medium does not equalise prices._

Full working after a second miss:

> The sellers no longer need matching immediate wants.  
> So the answer is “It separates the sale from the later purchase.”.  

> A seller keeps a balance for next week. What qualification is needed?

- ✅ Its later purchasing power may change.
- ◻️ It must buy exactly the same basket forever.  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: Price changes can alter the basket._
- ◻️ It proves the seller owns no other wealth.  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: A money balance says nothing complete about other assets._
- ◻️ It becomes worthless by definition overnight.  
  _↳ feedback if chosen: A possibility of change is not inevitable worthlessness._

Full working after a second miss:

> Store of value is not a guarantee of constant real value.  
> So the answer is “Its later purchasing power may change.”.  

Sources: money (Original CFA-franc cases and computed records; IMF money/inflation explanations; Federal Reserve price-level education; Bank of England bank-money mechanism; OpenStax bank balance-sheet and stylised multiplier; official BEAC institutional role)

### `n5-1-repair` · Lesson 1 · spot the error, level 3

Prompt: Find the incorrect economic claim.

Shows 3 correct and 1 wrong statement(s), drawn from:

- ✅ medium of exchange: an accepted intermediary used in payment.
- ✅ unit of account: a common measure for expressing prices and debts.
- ✅ store of value: a way of carrying purchasing ability over time.
- ✅ standard of deferred payment: a unit for stating an obligation payable later.
- ❌ Barter always requires no matching of wants.  
  _↳ Direct barter can require a double coincidence of wants._
- ❌ A common price unit guarantees identical prices for every good.  
  _↳ It allows comparison, not identical valuations._
- ❌ Money’s purchasing power can never change.  
  _↳ Price changes can alter what a fixed balance buys._
- ❌ A future nominal amount guarantees an unchanged real burden.  
  _↳ The real burden can change with the price level._


Three generated variants:

> Find the incorrect economic claim.

- ◻️ standard of deferred payment: a unit for stating an obligation payable later.  
  _↳ explanation: This is correct: a unit for stating an obligation payable later. Look for a statement that contradicts the lesson’s distinction._
- ✅ A common price unit guarantees identical prices for every good.  
  _↳ explanation: It allows comparison, not identical valuations._
- ◻️ medium of exchange: an accepted intermediary used in payment.  
  _↳ explanation: This is correct: an accepted intermediary used in payment. Look for a statement that contradicts the lesson’s distinction._
- ◻️ unit of account: a common measure for expressing prices and debts.  
  _↳ explanation: This is correct: a common measure for expressing prices and debts. Look for a statement that contradicts the lesson’s distinction._

### `n5-13-repair` · Lesson 13 · spot the error, level 3

Prompt: Find the incorrect economic claim.

Shows 3 correct and 1 wrong statement(s), drawn from:

- ✅ paired creation: a loan asset and deposit liability created together at origination.
- ✅ borrower debt: the obligation matching the borrower’s new loan funds.
- ✅ principal repayment: a reduction of loan and deposit through the stated repayment entry.
- ✅ money versus wealth: the distinction between a monetary balance and net resources.
- ❌ A bank records only a free asset when it credits the borrower.  
  _↳ It also owes the new deposit._

### `n5-19-repair` · Lesson 19 · spot the error, level 3

Prompt: Find the incorrect economic claim.

Shows 3 correct and 1 wrong statement(s), drawn from:

- ✅ public revenue: tax and non-tax receipts included under the stated classification.
- ✅ public expenditure: spending included under the stated public-budget classification.
- ✅ budget balance: revenue less expenditure for the stated period.
- ✅ stock-flow distinction: separation of a debt position from a period’s deficit.
- ❌ Borrowing is automatically a tax receipt in this model.  
  _↳ It is financing rather than classified revenue here._
- ❌ Every expenditure is money earned by government.  
  _↳ Spending and revenue have different roles._
- ❌ Adding expenditure to revenue gives this balance.  
  _↳ Expenditure is subtracted._
- ❌ An annual deficit and total debt are always the same amount.
